Goal Scorer Picks · 2026-11-25

Data: MoneyPuck game files (+ box scores), NHL schedule, Daily Faceoff goalies and lines. Chance = our calibrated chance of 1+ goals, from stats only. Edge = the checked chance (ours, blended with the books where that has proven better) minus the price's break-even chance, in points; 2+ is a bet.

What the model is good at

32 graded nights (2026-10-23 to 2026-11-24): 1198 players scored, model expected 1186. Every value bet (edge 2+ at major sportsbooks): 204-812, +85.3 units, ROI +8%. Suggested picks

Only edges where we've been right. A pick needs all three: (1) a spot where, when we've rated players above the books before, they scored closer to our number than the books' and our edge bets made money (re-checked every DAILY RESULTS run; right now: Defensemen, Defensemen, no PP, 2nd-line forwards, 2nd-liners on PP1, Defensemen on PP1, 2nd-liners on PP2, 3rd-liners, no PP); (2) our chance 15%+ above the books' consensus; (3) 2+ points of edge at the best price at major sportsbooks; (4) support from the data: the player gets many chances (0.21+ xG a game) or many high-danger shots (0.18+ a game), or the opponent is a weak defense (top third in expected goals allowed). Without (4) these picks won 2 of 60 over Nov 7-16. Not suggested, because the books have been right there: 1st-line forwards, 3rd-line forwards, 4th-line forwards.

1st period

Chance each team scores at least once in the 1st period tonight. Built from overall strength (half our model's expected team goals tonight, half the team's goals for x the opponent's goals against this season), scaled to the 1st period. A team's own 1st-period record didn't predict its next games in testing (it's shown for reference). Tested on 334 team-games: when we said 65%+, 72% scored; under 55%, 46%. League: 62% of teams score in the 1st; 29.1% of all goals come in the 1st. Scoreboards through 2026-11-24.

Matchup board

Model goals = the team's expected goals from tonight's skaters. Offense and defense grades rank all 32 teams on expected goals for and against (A = top 10%), blended with last season (offense 12 games, defense 25: defense this season settles slowly). Form = goals in the last 5 vs the team's normal (hot 15%+ above, cold 15%+ below). Power play = this team's power play (expected goals per 60 on the PP) vs the opponent's penalty kill (expected goals allowed per 60 shorthanded), graded across the league, and how many penalties the opponent takes a game; 5v5 xG% = share of expected goals at 5-on-5, this team vs the opponent. Those two come from MoneyPuck's team file and are context: tested game by game, they didn't improve the chances or reliably sort bets. Team numbers from MoneyPuck files through Nov 22.

Game plans (coach's view)

Each team's plan of attack: where the other team gives up shots and where its goalie leaks (NHL Edge shot locations, this season, pulled toward league average on small samples), special teams, form, and the players whose shot maps fit tonight's soft spots. Scouting context: the model already prices shot zones, and extra zone detail hasn't made the chances more accurate in testing, so the picks keep their tested rules.

Us vs the books

Ours = the model's own chance from stats only. Books = the sportsbooks' consensus with their margin taken out. Checked = the chance used for edges: for each group (defensemen, forwards) the self-check tests every day whether blending in the books predicts better than our chance alone, night by night, and blends only where it does. So far we've beaten the books on defensemen, and the books have beaten us on forwards.
